Use whenever the user says "wait for PR X to merge", "wait for PRs X and Y to merge", "watch these PRs until they merge", "let me know when PR X lands", "poll PR X", or invokes /wait-for-pr-merge. This is post-READY watching only — it does NOT fix CI, conflicts, or review comments (that is "the loop"; see `ci-loop`).
